# scripts/testing/check_new_test_failures.py --emit-baseline.
# This is the first run where shard 1 completed (PR #117 fixed the mimetypes/
# builtins.open hang that truncated it). Net vs the previous baseline: 65 failures
# fixed (PRs #113/#117/#119), 2 newly captured (order-dependent). 2299 entries.
# Includes test methods AND fixture-hook failures (setUpClass/setUpModule/...).
# Consumed by the checker: a PR fails the gate only if it produces a failing test
